| BIG Bangunan LOD 1, Makassar (de-duplicated by OBJECTID), box 119.40..119.45 x -5.16..-5.11 (Overture box has 80,773 buildings) | 106,440 (all with NDSMHMAX; median 8.7 m, 90th percentile 13.6 m, maximum 121.7 m) | 38,604 (36.3 %) by centroid in footprint; 15,630 of those fall in a footprint already used by another source feature (split or merged buildings); in a 3,000-feature sample the nearest Overture footprint is 1.1 m away at the median and 85 % are within 10 m | 16 = 0.04 % of matched (all other (OSM record)) | 38,588 = 36.3 % of source (a looser radius rule would raise it; not run) | 16 pairs: median abs diff 5.06 m, median signed (source minus Overture) -4.57 m, median ratio 0.54, within 20 % 0.0 % | Overture: 4 row groups of 2026-10-21.0 (compressed bytes not measured) + source: 435 leaf nodes (one Draco geometry and the OBJECTID and NDSMHMAX attribute buffers each; bytes not measured) |

The other direction (per Overture building):

| Source and box | Overture buildings in box | With a height (floors) | Height-less Overture buildings that gain a source height | Comparison |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| BIG Bangunan LOD 1, Makassar | 80,773 | 10 (0.01 %); floors on 610 (0.8 %) | 23,024 of the 23,031 Overture buildings that contain at least one source centroid (28.5 % of all 80,773) gain a height | not meaningful (16 pairs only, above); source heights look larger than Overture's few OSM heights, but the field meaning is unverified |

What the sources' values mean: the NUS height is the lab's extrusion estimate from HDB storeys (not a survey), so its comparison with OSM levels reduces to the same storey counts; BIG's NDSMHMAX is a surface-minus-terrain maximum from the 2024 mapping (my reading), so it can include roof furniture and trees on small houses; 8.7 m median for Makassar houses is high for mostly one- and two-storey roofs, so treat the values as unvalidated.

**Jakarta (footprint and floor counts, not matched)**

Counts by envelope query on the Master_Bangunan_View service, against the Overture boxes of the OVERTURE block (release 2026-10-21.0): box 106.80..106.85 x -6.20..-6.15: 100,473 source polygons, of which 737 have a floor count above 0 (Overture: 95,548 buildings, 41 heights); box 106.82..106.87 x -6.22..-6.17: 98,328 polygons, 792 with a floor count. The polygons were not matched to Overture footprints (no centroid test, no download of the 1.74 M polygons), so there is no measured share of Overture buildings that gain a floor count; the source's floor field is empty (0) on 99.35 % of its city-wide polygons, and no source field is a height in metres except in the 12-kelurahan roof-form set (62,223 parts, BLDGHEIGHT mean 6.85 m) and the Kota Tua scene layer (25,165 buildings, measuredHeight mean 10.1 m), neither overlap-tested. Balikpapan's 185,364 footprints have no height field.

### 11.4 South Asia and the Middle East

Method as the Europe-E report's section 10: the source building's representative point (polygon) or centroid (Beer Sheva's datastore) inside an Overture footprint of release 2026-10-21.0; "adds" = the source has a storey count and the matched Overture building has neither a height nor num_floors, or nothing matches. Overture and source both read live, no files kept.

| Source and box | Source buildings | Matched to an Overture footprint | Matched with an Overture height (by supplier) | Source buildings that add a height or storeys Overture lacks | Height comparison where both | Bytes read |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| Beer Sheva buildings (data.gov.il buildings-br7), centre box 34.77..34.82 x 31.23..31.28 (Overture box holds 11,998 buildings, 99 with a height, 738 with floors) | 16,034 with a centroid in the box (16,028 with floors above 0); floors 1 on 6,878, 2 on 5,257, 4 on 1,806 | 14,176 (88.4 %); 1,858 unmatched; several source points can fall in one Overture polygon | 110 = 0.8 % of matched (other (OSM record) 110) | 14,384 = 89.7 % of source | floors vs num_floors: 1,636 pairs, median abs diff 0, median signed 0, equal 68.0 %, within 1 floor 90.8 %; Overture height per source floor on 110 pairs: median 4.0 m | Overture 3.4 MB (1 row group) + source 7.4 MB JSON from the datastore API (all 26,350 rows) |
| Cyprus DLS buildings (curated GeoPackage), Nicosia box 33.34..33.39 x 35.15..35.20 (Overture box holds 26,896 buildings, 678 with a height, 2,217 with floors) | 16,321 BU3 polygons with a representative point in the box (16,320 with floors above 0); floors 1 on 6,671 | 10,702 (65.6 %); 5,619 unmatched | 426 = 4.0 % of matched (other (OSM record) 426) | 14,766 = 90.5 % of source | floors vs num_floors: 1,433 pairs, median abs diff 1, median signed +1 (source higher), equal 18.7 %, within 1 floor 52.1 %; Overture height per source floor on 426 pairs: median 2.3 m | Overture 12.3 MB (3 row groups) + source 285 MB GeoPackage (whole file, 563,446 buildings, read through its R-tree) |

What the source's value means: Abu Dhabi and Cyprus give storey counts of every kind of building (Abu Dhabi from permits and photogrammetry, Cyprus from 2014-2016 cadastral photogrammetry with a suspicious majority of "1"); Beer Sheva is indicative only per its own notes. None gives a metric height; a map using them would convert floors to metres with a stated rule. Agreement with Overture's own (OSM) floors is highest where the municipality keeps the data current (Beer Sheva 68 % exact) and lowest in Cyprus.

## 12. Unverified (routes tried are short)

### 12.1 East Asia (Japan, Koreas, Taiwan, Hong Kong, Macau)

- South Korea, V-World (vworld.kr): www.vworld.kr answered HTTP 502 on the download pages and then gave no connection; Wayback's CDX listed captures of the pages and then returned "Temporarily Offline" before any capture could be fetched. The download pages and field lists for GIS건물통합정보, 건물통합정보_마스터, 연속수치지형도_건물, GIS건물일반집합정보, 일별 and 건축물연령정보 stayed unread, so whether they download anonymously (the data.go.kr records call them "기관자체에서 다운로드") is unknown; no account was created. Routes tried: data.go.kr dataset pages and JSON-LD records (read: licences, row counts, links), V-World pages (502), Wayback CDX (offline), nsdi.go.kr (no connection).
- South Korea, 국가공간정보포털 (nsdi.go.kr): no connection from this Mac; Seoul's S-Map 3D (smap.seoul.go.kr) was not read, so whether it has an open building-height file or API is unknown (only news items from search results mention an announced open API). The NGII 국토정보플랫폼 (map.ngii.go.kr) statistics-map download needs a login (read on the data.go.kr record).
- South Korea, Gangwon (data.gwd.go.kr) and Gyeongnam (www.gyeongnam.go.kr) portals did not answer from this Mac; the data.go.kr listing for them found nothing.
- Japan, GSI 基盤地図情報: the download needs a login (read on https://www.gsi.go.jp/kiban/); not downloaded, no account created. Whether the 建築物の外周線 files carry any height or storeys item is unread (the pages read describe none). Old fgd.gsi.go.jp is no longer the address (the page says the download address changed 2025-04-01).
- Japan, the data.go.jp aggregate catalogue (www.data.go.jp) did not answer; the replacement data.e-gov.go.jp answered but holds national ministries only. Individual prefectural open-data portals (for example Hyogo, Aichi, Shizuoka, Hokkaido own sites) were searched only through BODIK and G-spatial, not visited.
- Taiwan: the own open-data portals of New Taipei, Taichung, Kaohsiung and Tainan were not usable (data.ntpc.gov.tw 401 "Unauthorized", opendata.taichung.gov.tw 401 "NO_AUTH", data.kcg.gov.tw and opendata.tainan.gov.tw did not answer, data.tainan.gov.tw 404), so those cities' holdings are known only through data.gov.tw's catalogue API (queried) and Taichung's newdatacenter.taichung.gov.tw (reached through the link in the data.gov.tw record). TGOS and NLSC's 3D platform (3dmap.nlsc.gov.tw, whgis-nlsc.moi.gov.tw) did not answer; 3d-landp.kcg.gov.tw returned an empty page; whether NLSC's priced 三維建物模型 is also an open-data download is unread.
- Macau: the DSSCU site (www.dsscu.gov.mo, reads "JavaScript Required") and the GIS geoportal (www.gis.gov.mo, a viewer, 200) were not read, so a building layer held by DSSCU outside the open-data portal is neither confirmed nor ruled out; the open-data portal's own catalogue (1,377 datasets) was read in full through its search API and has none. Routes tried: the portals' home pages, the script of data.gov.mo (api base found), the API (read), the OSM wiki page.
- Hong Kong 3D Tiles API: needs a key by email; no email was sent. The Hong Kong iB1000 FGDB and 3D-BIT00 downloads sit behind the One-Stop System (not opened).
- South Korea, the metropolitan portals (data.busan.go.kr, data.daegu.go.kr, data.incheon.go.kr, data.ulsan.go.kr, bigdata.gwangju.go.kr, openlab.daejeon.go.kr, data.gg.go.kr, data.chungbuk.go.kr): addresses from data.go.kr's regional-portal list; searched only through data.go.kr's file search and web searches, not catalogue by catalogue. Seoul's catalogue was searched directly.
- North Korea: no source page found beyond the Zenodo record and OSM.

### 12.2 China, Mongolia and the global sets

- **Chinese portals whose hosts do not answer or are blocked** (routes tried: the host from the fetched list, plain fetch with the project's user agent, name resolution and TCP checks, WebFetch, Wayback CDX, which was offline for the run): Shanghai (412), Tianjin (403), Inner Mongolia (403), Chongqing, Fujian, Shandong, Guizhou, Ningxia, Hainan, Jiangxi, Shaanxi, Sichuan, Xinjiang and the cities Guangzhou's own host, Shenzhen's own host, Xiamen, Qingdao, Jinan, Ningbo, Wuhan, Chengdu, Guiyang, Harbin, Changsha. Their catalogues were not read. Provinces and cities with no address on any fetched page (Hebei, Shanxi, Liaoning, Jilin, Jiangsu, Anhui, Hubei, Hunan, Guangxi, Gansu, Qinghai, Xizang, Nanjing, Xi'an) were not tried. The national platform data.gov.cn and its local-platform list do not connect from here. Wayback captures of these hosts should be retried once the Archive is back, one request at a time.
- **Licences of the Guangdong and Zhejiang platforms**: no terms page was read for either; the entries carry no licence field. The Shenzhen full download, the Ruian full table (491 rows) and the Fuyang register were not downloaded (previews only), and whether the downloads need sign-in was not tested.
- **The Egger et al. "Shenzhen 3D building points" set**: not found on the Guangdong platform (the 建筑物数据 entry holds no points); Shenzhen's own host does not connect.
- **Tianditu** terms and any bulk download: blocked (418); bot block not circumvented. Wayback has no capture of the copyright page.
- **Real-scene 3D China progress figures** (310 cities, 110,000 km2) came from a search answer; the page timed out and is not cited.
- **Wuhan Wuchang district 3D map** (a search answer calls it a paid authorized-operation product): the news page I fetched does not mention it; unconfirmed.
- **GlobalBuildingAtlas content in China**: coverage was read from index files and one Mongolian tile was opened; no China tile was opened, so China's per-building counts, height completeness and the Overture comparison are not measured. HuggingFace (the ODbL and LoD1 parts' official host) was not used.
- **Mongolia**: ALAGaC's geoportal host (geoportal.nsdi.gov.mn) and its other hosts named on the agency page (point.gazar.gov.mn) do not connect; the geoportal's layers, and any Ulaanbaatar city portal, are unverified (a search found no live city portal address).
- **GABLE**: no dataset of that name found on Zenodo, figshare or by web search; whether it exists is unknown. GLAMOUR's China coverage and file location, and the SHAFTS reference cities, were not read beyond the Zenodo records. **Maxar and Esri's own 3D layers**: not examined. **Baidu 3D building layer** and the Egger et al. 40-city data: no download found.

### 12.3 South-East and Central Asia

- Indonesia, BIG licence and portal terms: Ina-Geoportal's terms and download rules (JavaScript app, account and bot-challenge key in the bundle; no account made), BIG regulations 5/2025 and 3/2024 (peraturan.go.id refused the connection; peraturan.bpk.go.id answered 403), the BIG token-protected folders (DATABASE, RBI_GORONTALO, RBI_SULBAR, RBI_SULTENG, RBI_SULUT, UJICOBA, Utilities); routes tried: BIG site product and service pages, the portal bundle, web search, the services directory. Jakarta: licence of the tataruang.jakarta.go.id services and Jakarta's open-data terms not found; the public 3D layer "Building2" and the folders bangunan_Tinggi_Dinas_Sudin, akses_shp_file and imb need a token (not approached); the 1.74 M polygons were not downloaded or matched. Provinces: Maluku's and Bali's catalogues are script-driven; the homepages listed in Nothing better that did not answer were tried once each (Wikidata site addresses); Banda Aceh's and Bandung's data portals not searched.
- Malaysia: JUPEM's 3D model of Kuala Lumpur and Putrajaya (only a paper with a JUPEM co-author read; no data page found); Selangor (SelGDX host not found in the pages read; selangor policy PDF not read), Penang (land-office open-data page timed out; PEGIS and JPBD GeoPortal known only from search snippets), Sabah, Kuala Lumpur (DBKL): hosts timed out or answered a block page; routes tried: the portals' main hosts, state sites that did answer, news pages (one Cloudflare 403), the Internet Archive availability API (Penang 2019 copy only).
- Thailand: data.go.th (403 Access Denied) and the live Bangkok portal (data.bangkok.go.th does not connect; opendata.bangkok.go.th is a script shell): the archived Bangkok CKAN (2025-04-24, 1,139 datasets) has no search pages, so building (อาคาร) could not be searched; DOL, DPT and RTSD (rtsd.mi.th did not connect).
- Vietnam (Hanoi, HCMC, data.gov.vn, MONRE), Uzbekistan (data.gov.uz: archive captures of 2022 not opened because the Internet Archive went offline), Tajikistan, Turkmenistan, Laos, Timor-Leste, Almaty and Astana city portals: the portals did not connect or blocked; Internet Archive: nothing for opendata.hanoi.gov.vn and data.gov.tj, 2012 captures only for data.gov.vn.
- Philippines: the Geoportal Philippines WFS needs a login; the WMS "Built-up Area" layers were listed, not fetched; LiPAD is request-only and its live site did not connect; data.gov.ph is a script shell with no API found; Myanmar's MIMU not tried.
- Microsoft ML Buildings and 3D-GloBFP licence text: not fetched here (global agent).

## 13. Accidental

### 13.1 East Asia (Japan, Koreas, Taiwan, Hong Kong, Macau)

- Output of a script printed the base64 body of one CKAN data-URI resource (PLATEAU Chuo-ku 2025 catalogue file) into the terminal: nothing sensitive, only noise.
- A first BODIK/G-spatial search used wrong CKAN syntax and returned empty sets (no harm); data.gov.tw's list API ignores a plain `keyword` or `qs` field (it returned the whole 52,595-dataset catalogue, then 0 hits for a wrong body) and answers only to the body its own page sends (`bool: [{fulltext: {value}}]`, found by reading the site's script); data.gov.tw's HTML search pages are client-rendered.
- Useful API finds: PLATEAU's catalogue API https://api.plateauview.mlit.go.jp/datacatalog/plateau-datasets (9.7 MB JSON, 7,776 datasets, spec, year, LOD, format per dataset); G-spatial CKAN (17,332 datasets, `package_search` caps at 1,000 rows per call and about 4,000 in total over pages); PLATEAU CityGML zips honour HTTP range requests, so `remotezip` pulls single tiles from 1.5-5 GB zips; Hong Kong's CSDI WFS answers anonymously with GeoJSON and `startIndex` paging (cap 10,000); data.go.kr dataset pages are server-rendered and give rows, extension, update date and licence type in plain HTML (`https://www.data.go.kr/data/<id>/fileData.do`), and `https://www.data.go.kr/catalog/<id>/openapi.json` gives a JSON-LD record; data.taipei exposes `dataset.view?id=` JSON and `resource.download?rid=`. Wayback went offline mid-run.
- One zip (Oita) extracted with unzip failed on Shift-JIS names; I listed it instead and read shx headers.
- Python's urllib could not connect to www.gsi.go.jp ("unsafe legacy renegotiation"); curl read the same pages. Downloads made and deleted: Seoul solar zip 161 MB, Taichung 7z 78 MB, Shizuoka (Mishima) land-use zip 258 MB, Gwacheon zip 1 MB, a 160 MB partial of the Shizuoka zip (aborted, restarted).
- Useful finds this run: data.go.kr file downloads need no login (`/tcs/dss/selectFileDataDownload.do` then `/cmm/cmm/fileDownload.do?atchFileId=..&fileDetailSn=1`, `check-limit.json` said no captcha); data.seoul.go.kr files are a form POST to datafile.seoul.go.kr; Macau's catalogue API is POST https://api.data.gov.mo/datadir/search (page 0..2 of 500); Taichung's Drive-shared 7z needs py7zr; Hong Kong's WFS needs paging with startIndex above 10,000 features.
- A `ps` listing showed other agents' Python processes on this Mac; none touched, none mine left running.

### 13.2 China, Mongolia and the global sets

- A first extraction pass used shell `unzip` on archives with Chinese folder names and wrote a few failed directories into my own scratch; and an `rm -f data/*/*.zip` I meant for after extraction ran early and deleted the three downloads, which I re-downloaded (about 650 MB extra, one extra pass). No repo or other agents' files touched.
- Useful finds about APIs: figshare's the figshare article API (api.figshare.com/v2/articles/ followed by the article id) returns the file list with `download_url` and licence for each 3D-GloBFP part (ten parts, about 198 GB) and for CMAB; ndownloader.figshare.com links download without login. Zenodo's concept DOIs (11397014 and others) redirect to the new record numbers (15487006 and others). The Microsoft location index `dataset-links.csv` gives every country's tile count in one fetch. Overture's anonymous S3 listing works for any release prefix and footers of 512 parts took about 45 s with 16 threads.
- The Wayback CDX returns 200 captures reliably but refuses connections after a short burst of parallel requests: keep to one request at a time with a few seconds between.
- Useful finds about APIs: the Guangdong platform's search is POST /backOpen/open/data/data/openCatalog/findPageBySearch (base path in /config.js, JSON keys pageNum, pageSize, keyword); Zhejiang's is POST /dopServer/dataCatalog/categoryQueryPage with key dataName (ten entries a page; other key names are silently ignored) and its preview is a POST to .../catalogDataPreview/{url-encoded id}; Beijing's is POST /cms/web/search, which ignores keyword fields but accepts pageSize=1000, so the whole catalogue is five calls; Mongolia's is GET /apiv1/dataset-file (all 738 entries in one answer). The GlobalBuildingAtlas mediaTUM record prints public rsync and FTP credentials for its representative share (index files only, 8 GB listed): only the three index files and the README were fetched; the Source Cooperative bucket lists anonymously over https and its per-tile parquet files can be read without credentials.
- A few of my first Beijing and Zhejiang search calls printed whole pages of unfiltered titles because the keyword field was ignored; nothing was changed outside my scratch folder.

### 13.3 South-East and Central Asia

- The Overture heights for Singapore are 100 % OSM records, so the 12.4 % share in the Singapore centre box is the Singapore mappers' work, and the lab's 2019 model used the same OSM input; the storey agreement of 92.6 % in the Toa Payoh overlap confirms it.
- data.gov.sg: the `query` parameter of /v2/public/api/datasets is ignored (all datasets come back regardless); the full list is 468 pages of results. The v1 poll-download API returns a signed S3 URL that works for 1 hour.
- BIG's ArcGIS server lets the REST directory and the I3S scene layers be read without a token; the Draco geometry decodes with DracoPy, and the position vertices are metres from the node centre (obb centre in longitude and latitude).
- www.tashkent.uz returns 403 for Canadian IPs ("Your country is not allowed to access this site").
- The Internet Archive `id_` captures are gzip-compressed bytes; decompress them.
- Jakarta's ArcGIS Server (tataruang.jakarta.go.id/server) lists its whole services directory and answers building queries without a token for Master_Bangunan, while the layer the public 3D map uses (Hosted/Building2) is token-protected; its address was found only in the 3D page's JavaScript, not on any portal page.
- Satu Data Jakarta's search is a POST API: parameter "q" filters, the other names tried ("keyword", "search", "query") return the unfiltered list, and "page_size" is ignored (8 per page).
- BIG's I3S statistics endpoints (layers/0/statistics/f_N/0) give feature totals and histograms without decoding geometry; the f_N numbers differ between layers (map them through the layer's statisticsInfo names), and Sulsel_Bangunan_LOD_1's DSMHMAX and DTMH statistics are corrupted by 1.8e308 sentinel values.
- Internet Archive: the CDX search lists Bangkok's CKAN captures but the service went offline ("Temporarily Offline") after the Bangkok check, so the Uzbek captures of 2022 and any further captures were not opened.
- No account was created, no login or challenge was bypassed. The BIG and Jakarta token-protected folders were left alone.

### 13.4 South Asia and the Middle East

- data.gov.il CloudFront answers 403 to the e.data.gov.il file downloads (GeoJSON, CSV) from here, while the CKAN `datastore_search` API on data.gov.il returns the same rows; I used the API, which is the published route, and did not try to get round the block.
- data.gov.il's `package_search` with `fq=organization:israel_mapping_center&rows=100` lists an organization's datasets; Hebrew `q=` terms often give 0 hits.
- ADSDI's ArcGIS REST serves f=geojson with an envelope filter and outSR=4326; paging with resultOffset works at 2000 a page (3 requests for one box).
- Bahrain's data.gov.bh is an OpenDataSoft portal: `/api/explore/v2.1/catalog/datasets?where=search(dataset_id,"build")` works.
- Abu Dhabi's Open Data Platform (data.abudhabi) is a script-built DKAN front end; its Drupal JSON:API (`/opendata/jsonapi/node/open_data_license`, also `faqs`, `about_us`, `privacy_policy`) answers anonymously with the page texts. Its DKAN search and metastore endpoints (`/opendata/api/1/search`, `/opendata/api/1/metastore/schemas/dataset/items`) timed out or returned HTTP 500.
- Pune's open data portal is a Next.js site whose datasets API is `https://opendataapi.pmc.gov.in/datasets?size=500&page=0` (all 238 datasets in one page, JSON, no key); a search parameter is ignored.
- Opendatasoft portals (Qatar, Ajman, Bahrain): the `where=search(dataset_id,"term") or search(title,"term")` form works for title and id; the plain `search("term")` form returned HTTP 400 on Qatar's portal.
- Many Indian government hosts (state remote sensing centres, data.gov.in, Survey of India's geoportal, the MP and Kerala geoportals) refuse connections outright (connection refused at the TCP level, on both curl and WebFetch); Wayback captures read fine. Saudi government hosts and Dubai Pulse behave the same.
- Python in the venv needed certifi (requests) for HTTPS to the Abu Dhabi host; urllib alone failed certificate verification.
- Nothing destructive; no accounts, forms or logins were used. One long title-check loop ran past the 2 minute limit and was backgrounded; it finished by itself.
